# Code Review: Subagent Implementation (feat/subagent-sql-chart)

## Summary

This branch replaces MCP tool-based `execute_sql`/`generate_chart` with two specialized subagents (`sql-analyst`, `chart-builder`) using the claude-agent-sdk `AgentDefinition` API. The implementation spans 14 files across 8 commits and follows the design plan closely.

---

## Strengths

**1. Clean separation of concerns.** The `build_subagent_definitions()` function at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` (lines 89-143) is well-structured -- it builds `AgentDefinition` objects with clear prompts, correct tool assignments, and configurable models. The `_build_table_schemas()` helper properly extracts a reusable text representation of schemas.

**2. Thorough removal of `generate_chart`.** The `generate_chart` tool was cleanly excised from all three locations where it existed: `tools.py`, `mcp_sse.py`, and the system prompt. No orphaned references remain in the frontend. Tests were updated to explicitly verify the tool is gone (e.g., `test_server_has_only_execute_sql_tool` at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/tests/test_tools.py:50-54`).

**3. Both code paths updated consistently.** The subagent SSE events (`subagent_start`/`subagent_end`) are emitted in both the subprocess mode (`stream_chat` at lines 610-668) and the container mode (`_stream_chat_container` at lines 357-419) in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py`. This is critical for production parity.

**4. Well-defined TypeScript types.** The new `SubagentEvent` and extended `ContentSegment` types at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/types.ts` (lines 38-59) properly model the new data flow with correct optional fields.

**5. Good test coverage for core logic.** The test files `test_subagent_definitions.py` (5 tests), `test_agent_subagent_sse.py` (2 tests), `test_subagent_config.py` (1 test), and `test_agent_chart.py` (1 test) verify the key contracts: agent definitions, SSE event emission, config defaults, and system prompt delegation.

**6. Incremental, well-structured commits.** The 8 commits follow a logical bottom-up order matching the plan, making the change reviewable and bisectable.

---

## Issues

### Important (Should Fix)

**I-1: Stale comment referencing `generate_chart`.** At `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` line 647, the comment reads:

```python
# Try to parse JSON output (e.g. chart_spec from generate_chart)
```

This references a tool that no longer exists. It should be updated to reference the chart-builder subagent instead, e.g.:

```python
# Try to parse JSON output (e.g. chart_spec from chart-builder subagent)
```

**I-2: `_build_table_schemas()` has a subtle message difference from the plan.** The plan's implementation at design doc line 161 says the empty-tables message should be `"\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."` but the actual implementation at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` line 80 returns just `"\nNo tables are currently loaded."` (without the upload instruction). This is a minor plan deviation. For the subagent prompts, omitting the upload instruction is arguably correct since subagents don't interact with users about uploads, so this is actually a reasonable improvement over the plan. However, it should be a conscious decision -- if it was unintentional, it should be reconciled.

**I-3: `SubagentEvent` type is defined but never imported or used.** The `SubagentEvent` interface at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/types.ts` lines 38-47 is exported but never imported anywhere in the codebase. The frontend SSE handler and AgentContext both use inline type definitions for the callback parameters instead. This is dead code. Either remove it or use it in the callback signatures to reduce inline type duplication.

**I-4: Large callback duplication in `AgentContext.tsx`.** The `onSubagentStart` and `onSubagentEnd` callback implementations are copy-pasted identically between `sendMessage` (lines 170-209) and `editMessage` (lines 376-415) in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx`. This was already a pattern in the existing code for other callbacks (`onToolCall`, `onToolResult`, etc.), but this PR extends the duplication further. While not a regression (it follows the existing pattern), this is growing technical debt. The shared callback object construction should ideally be extracted to a helper function to reduce the risk of future divergence between the two paths.

**I-5: Hardcoded English strings in `MessageBubble.tsx` instead of using i18n.** At `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx` lines 72-76:

```tsx
const displayName = seg.subagentName === 'sql-analyst'
  ? 'SQL Analyst working...'
  : seg.subagentName === 'chart-builder'
  ? 'Chart Builder working...'
  : `${seg.subagentName} working...`;
```

The design plan (line 949-952) actually suggested using `t('sqlAnalystWorking')` with a fallback: `t('sqlAnalystWorking') || 'SQL Analyst working...'`. The existing codebase uses `useTranslation()` for all user-facing text (the `t()` function is already imported and used in this same component). These strings should use the translation system for consistency.

### Minor (Nice to Have)

**M-1: Container mode detects subagent results by name, not by tool.** In `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py` lines 408 and 657, the subagent result detection checks:

```python
if name in ("sql-analyst", "chart-builder"):
```

This is a hard-coded list of subagent names. If a new subagent is added in the future, this list must be updated in four places (two in container mode, two in subprocess mode). A more robust approach would be to compare against the keys of `build_subagent_definitions(db)` or track which tool_use_ids came from Task invocations. However, since the subagent set is small and stable, this is acceptable for now.

**M-2: The `chart_spec` field uses snake_case in TypeScript types.** The `chart_spec` field at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/types.ts` lines 32-35 and 43-46 and 55-58 uses Python-style `snake_case` naming, which is inconsistent with TypeScript's conventional `camelCase` (the same interface uses `toolCallId`, `toolName`, `rowCount`, etc.). This is an existing pattern inherited from the JSON wire format, and changing it now would require a mapper layer, so it is not actionable for this PR but worth noting.

**M-3: Test `test_subagent_models_use_config` could be more specific.** At `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/tests/test_subagent_definitions.py` lines 58-66:

```python
def test_subagent_models_use_config():
    agents = build_subagent_definitions(db)
    assert agents["sql-analyst"].model is not None
    assert agents["chart-builder"].model is not None
```

This only asserts `is not None`, which would pass even if the model were set to an arbitrary string. A stronger assertion would verify the actual default value:

```python
assert agents["sql-analyst"].model == "haiku"
assert agents["chart-builder"].model == "haiku"
```

Or parametrize with env var overrides to verify the config wiring works end-to-end.

---

## Assessment

**Plan Alignment: Excellent.** All 11 implementation tasks from the design plan (Tasks 1-11) are faithfully implemented. The commit history maps 1:1 to the plan's task breakdown. The two minor deviations identified (I-2 empty-table message simplification, I-5 i18n fallback omission) are both defensible and low-impact.

**Correctness: Good.** The SSE event flow for `subagent_start` and `subagent_end` is correctly wired in both backend code paths (subprocess and container) and correctly handled in the frontend's SSE parser, state management, and rendering. The `chart_spec` extraction from subagent results follows the same parsing pattern used for the old `generate_chart` tool results, ensuring backward compatibility for chart rendering.

**Code Quality: Good with caveats.** The implementation is clean and follows existing patterns well. The main quality concern is the callback duplication in `AgentContext.tsx` (I-4), which is pre-existing technical debt that this PR extends rather than introduces. The stale comment (I-1) is a small oversight that should be fixed before merge.

**Security: No new concerns.** The subagent definitions don't expose any new attack surface. The `mcp__duckdb__execute_sql` tool permission is properly scoped. The `session_token` lifecycle management is unchanged.

**Test Coverage: Adequate.** The 9 new/modified tests cover the core contracts (definitions, config, SSE emission, system prompt). The gap is that there are no tests for `subagent_end` event emission (only `subagent_start` is tested in `test_agent_subagent_sse.py`), and no frontend unit tests for the new rendering logic in `MessageBubble.tsx`. However, the design plan's Task 12-13 explicitly deferred E2E verification to manual testing.

**Verdict: Approve with minor fixes.** Items I-1 (stale comment) and I-5 (i18n strings) should be addressed before merge. Items I-3 (dead type) and I-4 (callback duplication) are worth tracking as follow-up tasks but should not block this PR.

> AGENT

**Root cause identified.** Lines 660-665: The code does `json.loads(output)` on the subagent's result text. But the chart-builder subagent returns **markdown text with an embedded JSON code block**, not pure JSON. For example:

```
Here's the visualization:

```json
{"chart_spec": {"data": [...], "layout": {...}}}
```

The chart shows revenue by product.
```

`json.loads()` fails on this markdown, so it falls through to `end_data["result"] = output` — the raw text including the JSON blob. The orchestrator then echoes this in its answer text, and the frontend shows raw JSON.

**Phase 2: Pattern Analysis.** The fix needs to extract `chart_spec` from markdown-wrapped JSON. A regex to find ````json\n{...}\n``` ` blocks is the right approach.

**Phase 3: Hypothesis.** Add a helper function that extracts chart_spec from text — first try `json.loads()` (pure JSON), then fall back to regex extraction from markdown code blocks.

Let me implement the fix.

   162→def _extract_tool_result_text(content: object) -> str:
   163→    """Extract text from ToolResultBlock.content."""
   164→    if content is None:
   165→        return ""
   166→    if isinstance(content, str):
   167→        return content
   168→    if isinstance(content, list):
   169→        parts = []
   170→        for item in content:
   171→            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
   172→                parts.append(item.get("text", ""))
   173→        return "\n".join(parts)
   174→    return str(content)

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py",
  "old_string": "def _extract_tool_result_text(content: object) -> str:",
  "new_string": "def _extract_chart_spec(text: str) -> dict | None:\n    \"\"\"Extract chart_spec from subagent output text.\n\n    The chart-builder subagent returns markdown containing a JSON code block.\n    Try pure JSON first, then extract from ```json ... ``` fenced blocks.\n    \"\"\"\n    import re\n\n    # Try pure JSON first\n    try:\n        parsed = json.loads(text)\n        if \"chart_spec\" in parsed:\n            return parsed[\"chart_spec\"]\n    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):\n        pass\n\n    # Extract from markdown fenced code blocks (```json ... ``` or ``` ... ```)\n    pattern = r\"```(?:json)?\\s*\\n?(.*?)\\n?\\s*```\"\n    for match in re.finditer(pattern, text, re.DOTALL):\n        block = match.group(1).strip()\n        try:\n            parsed = json.loads(block)\n            if \"chart_spec\" in parsed:\n                return parsed[\"chart_spec\"]\n            # The block itself might BE the chart_spec\n            if \"data\" in parsed and isinstance(parsed[\"data\"], list):\n                return parsed\n        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):\n            continue\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef _extract_tool_result_text(content: object) -> str:"
}

    29→# Monkey-patch parse_message to handle unknown message types (e.g. rate_limit_event)
    30→# gracefully instead of crashing the stream. The SDK (v0.1.39) doesn't recognize
    31→# newer message types from the CLI. Returning a SystemMessage lets the stream
    32→# continue since our code ignores SystemMessage instances.
    33→import claude_agent_sdk._internal.message_parser as _parser
    34→
    35→_original_parse_message = _parser.parse_message
    36→
    37→
    38→def _safe_parse_message(data):
    39→    try:
    40→        return _original_parse_message(data)
    41→    except MessageParseError as e:
    42→        if "Unknown message type" in str(e):
    43→            msg_type = data.get("type", "unknown") if isinstance(data, dict) else "unknown"
    44→            logger.warning("Skipping unrecognized message type from CLI: %s", msg_type)
    45→            return SystemMessage(subtype=msg_type, data=data if isinstance(data, dict) else {})
    46→        raise
    47→
    48→
    49→_parser.parse_message = _safe_parse_message
    50→
    51→
    52→def build_system_prompt(db: Database) -> str:
    53→    tables = db.list_tables()
    54→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    55→
    56→- Use the sql-analyst agent for any data question that requires SQL queries
    57→- Use the chart-builder agent for any visualization, chart, or graph request
    58→- Explain findings in plain language after getting results
    59→
    60→Identity:
    61→- You are an AI assistant. If asked whether you are an AI or a human, always confirm that you are an AI.
    62→- Do not disclose the name, version, or provider of the underlying language model.
    63→"""
    64→    if not tables:
    65→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    66→    else:
    67→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    68→        for table in tables:
    69→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    70→            for col in table["columns"]:
    71→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    72→
    73→    return prompt
    74→
    75→
    76→def _build_table_schemas(db: Database) -> str:
    77→    """Build a text description of all loaded table schemas for subagent prompts."""
    78→    tables = db.list_tables()
    79→    if not tables:
    80→        return "\nNo tables are currently loaded."
    81→    text = "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    82→    for table in tables:
    83→        text += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    84→        for col in table["columns"]:
    85→            text += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    86→    return text
    87→
    88→
    89→def build_subagent_definitions(db: Database) -> dict[str, AgentDefinition]:
    90→    """Build AgentDefinition objects for the sql-analyst and chart-builder subagents."""
    91→    table_schemas = _build_table_schemas(db)
    92→
    93→    sql_prompt = (
    94→        "You are a DuckDB SQL expert. Given a user's data question, write and execute "
    95→        "SQL queries to find the answer.\n\n"
    96→        "Guidelines:\n"
    97→        "- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries.\n"
    98→        "- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT).\n"
    99→        "- If a query fails, read the error message, fix the SQL, and retry.\n"
   100→        "- Use double quotes for table and column identifiers that might conflict "
   101→        "with reserved words.\n"
   102→        "- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results.\n"
   103→        + table_schemas
   104→    )
   105→
   106→    chart_prompt = (
   107→        "You are a data visualization expert using Plotly. Given a user's request for "
   108→        "a chart or visualization, query the data with SQL and then output a JSON code "
   109→        "block with a `chart_spec` object containing `data` (an array of Plotly traces) "
   110→        "and `layout`.\n\n"
   111→        "Guidelines:\n"
   112→        "- Choose the most appropriate chart type (bar, line, scatter, pie, histogram, "
   113→        "box, heatmap, etc.).\n"
   114→        "- For pie charts, use `labels` and `values` fields in the trace.\n"
   115→        "- For multi-series data, group into separate traces or use a `color` dimension.\n"
   116→        "- Always include a descriptive `title` in the layout.\n"
   117→        "- Keep the chart clean and readable.\n\n"
   118→        "Output format:\n"
   119→        "```json\n"
   120→        '{"chart_spec": {"data": [<plotly traces>], "layout": {<plotly layout>}}}\n'
   121→        "```\n"
   122→        + table_schemas
   123→    )
   124→
   125→    return {
   126→        "sql-analyst": AgentDefinition(
   127→            description=(
   128→                "Use this agent for any data question that requires SQL queries "
   129→                "— exploring data, aggregations, filtering, joins, etc."
   130→            ),
   131→            prompt=sql_prompt,
   132→            tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
   133→            model=SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL,
   134→        ),
   135→        "chart-builder": AgentDefinition(
   136→            description=(
   137→                "Use this agent when the user wants a chart, graph, or visualization."
   138→            ),
   139→            prompt=chart_prompt,
   140→            tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
   141→            model=CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL,
   142→        ),
   143→    }
   144→
   145→
   146→def _build_message_with_history(
   147→    message: str, conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None
   148→) -> str:
   149→    """Prepend conversation history context to the user message when editing."""
   150→    if not conversation_history:
   151→        return message
   152→
   153→    history_text = "Previous conversation (for context, I am now editing a message):\n"
   154→    for entry in conversation_history:
   155→        role = entry.get("role", "user").capitalize()
   156→        content = entry.get("content", "")
   157→        history_text += f"\n{role}: {content}\n"
   158→    history_text += f"\n---\n\nMy updated message:\n{message}"
   159→    return history_text
   160→
   161→
   162→def _extract_chart_spec(text: str) -> dict | None:
   163→    """Extract chart_spec from subagent output text.
   164→
   165→    The chart-builder subagent returns markdown containing a JSON code block.
   166→    Try pure JSON first, then extract from ```json ... ``` fenced blocks.
   167→    """
   168→    import re
   169→
   170→    # Try pure JSON first
   171→    try:
   172→        parsed = json.loads(text)
   173→        if "chart_spec" in parsed:
   174→            return parsed["chart_spec"]
   175→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
   176→        pass
   177→
   178→    # Extract from markdown fenced code blocks (```json ... ``` or ``` ... ```)
   179→    pattern = r"```(?:json)?\s*\n?(.*?)\n?\s*```"
   180→    for match in re.finditer(pattern, text, re.DOTALL):
   181→        block = match.group(1).strip()
   182→        try:
   183→            parsed = json.loads(block)
   184→            if "chart_spec" in parsed:
   185→                return parsed["chart_spec"]
   186→            # The block itself might BE the chart_spec
   187→            if "data" in parsed and isinstance(parsed["data"], list):
   188→                return parsed
   189→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
   190→            continue
   191→
   192→    return None
   193→
   194→
   195→def _extract_tool_result_text(content: object) -> str:
   196→    """Extract text from ToolResultBlock.content."""
   197→    if content is None:
   198→        return ""
   199→    if isinstance(content, str):
   200→        return content
   201→    if isinstance(content, list):
   202→        parts = []
   203→        for item in content:
   204→            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
   205→                parts.append(item.get("text", ""))
   206→        return "\n".join(parts)
   207→    return str(content)
   208→
   209→
   210→async def _stream_chat_container(
   211→    message: str,
   212→    session_id: str | None,
   213→    db: Database,
   214→    conversation_history: list[dict] | None,
   215→    container_manager,
   216→    backend_session_id: str | None = None,
   217→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,
   218→) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   219→    """Stream chat via containerized sidecar instead of local subprocess."""
   220→    import httpx
   221→    import asyncio
   222→
   223→    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history)
   224→    system_prompt = build_system_prompt(db)
   225→
   226→    session_token=[REDACTED].create_token()
   227→
   228→    # Pass Langfuse credentials to the container so the sidecar's
   229→    # TypeScript Langfuse SDK can create traces directly.
   230→    env: dict[str, str] = {
   231→        "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": session_token,
   232→        "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   233→    }
   234→    if LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
   235→        env["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY
   236→        env["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY
   237→        env["LANGFUSE_BASE_URL"] = LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
   238→
   239→    if "127.0.0.1" in PROXY_BASE_URL or "localhost" in PROXY_BASE_URL:
   240→        logger.warning(
   241→            "PROXY_BASE_URL=%s uses localhost which is unreachable from containers. "
   242→            "Set PROXY_BASE_URL to the host's Docker-accessible address "
   243→            "(e.g., http://host.docker.internal:10000).",
   244→            PROXY_BASE_URL,
   245→        )
   246→
   247→    # Use the backend session ID (X-Session-ID header) for both:
   248→    # 1. MCP SSE URL — so the container queries the correct DuckDB instance
   249→    # 2. Container lifecycle key — so the same container is reused across
   250→    #    requests from the same browser tab (the Claude agent session_id
   251→    #    changes after the first response, which would orphan the container)
   252→    stable_session = backend_session_id or session_id or "default"
   253→
   254→    try:
   255→        # Send SSE keepalive immediately so the HTTP response starts and
   256→        # intermediate proxies (Vite, nginx) don't drop the idle connection
   257→        # before we've finished the blocking Docker container creation.
   258→        yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   259→
   260→        # container_manager.create() is synchronous (blocking Docker API call).
   261→        # Run it in a thread executor so the event loop stays responsive and
   262→        # can continue flushing keepalives to the client during startup.
   263→        # gVisor (runsc) containers can take 10-30 seconds to spin up.
   264→        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   265→        create_future = loop.run_in_executor(None, container_manager.create, stable_session, env)
   266→
   267→        max_create_wait = 60.0
   268→        elapsed = 0.0
   269→        while not create_future.done():
   270→            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
   271→            elapsed += 2.0
   272→            if elapsed >= max_create_wait:
   273→                create_future.cancel()
   274→                raise RuntimeError(f"Container creation timed out after {max_create_wait:.0f}s")
   275→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   276→        info = await create_future
   277→
   278→        # Wait for container to be ready
   279→        for attempt in range(10):
   280→            try:
   281→                async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(5.0)) as check_client:
   282→                    resp = await check_client.get(f"{info.url}/health")
   283→                    if resp.status_code == 200:
   284→                        break
   285→            except Exception:
   286→                pass
   287→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   288→            await asyncio.sleep(1)
   289→        else:
   290→            raise RuntimeError("Sidecar container failed health check after 10 attempts")
   291→
   292→        payload: dict = {
   293→            "message": query_message,
   294→            "session_id": session_id,
   295→            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   296→            "model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   297→            "mcp_server_url": f"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/mcp/sse?session_id={stable_session}",
   298→            "env": {
   299→                "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": session_token,
   300→                "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   301→            },
   302→            "agents": {
   303→                name: {
   304→                    "description": agent_def.description,
   305→                    "prompt": agent_def.prompt,
   306→                    "tools": agent_def.tools,
   307→                    "model": agent_def.model,
   308→                }
   309→                for name, agent_def in build_subagent_definitions(db).items()
   310→            },
   311→        }
   312→        if langfuse_session_id:
   313→            payload["langfuse_session_id"] = langfuse_session_id
   314→        # Pass original message & history separately for Langfuse trace metadata
   315→        if conversation_history:
   316→            payload["original_message"] = message
   317→            payload["conversation_history"] = conversation_history
   318→
   319→        has_tool_calls = False
   320→        has_thinking = False
   321→        done_sent = False
   322→        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}
   323→        tool_sqls: dict[str, str] = {}
   324→        actual_session_id = session_id
   325→
   326→        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(300.0)) as client:
   327→            async with client.stream("POST", f"{info.url}/query", json=payload) as response:
   328→                async for line in response.aiter_lines():
   329→                    if not line.startswith("data: "):
   330→                        continue
   331→                    raw = line[6:]
   332→                    try:
   333→                        msg = json.loads(raw)
   334→                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
   335→                        continue
   336→
   337→                    msg_type = msg.get("type")
   338→
   339→                    # --- Token-level streaming events from SDK ---
   340→                    if msg_type == "stream_event":
   341→                        event = msg.get("event", {})
   342→                        event_type = event.get("type", "")
   343→
   344→                        if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   345→                            delta = event.get("delta", {})
   346→                            delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   347→                            if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   348→                                text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   349→                                if text:
   350→                                    yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   351→                            elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   352→                                text = delta.get("text", "")
   353→                                if text:
   354→                                    event_name = "answer" if has_tool_calls else "thinking"
   355→                                    yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   356→
   357→                        elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   358→                            block = event.get("content_block", {})
   359→                            block_type = block.get("type")
   360→                            if block_type == "thinking":
   361→                                has_thinking = True
   362→                            elif block_type == "text":
   363→                                if has_thinking:
   364→                                    yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   365→                            elif block_type == "tool_use":
   366→                                has_thinking = False
   367→                                has_tool_calls = True
   368→
   369→                    # --- Complete assistant message (contains tool_use blocks) ---
   370→                    elif msg_type == "assistant":
   371→                        message_obj = msg.get("message", {})
   372→                        for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
   373→                            block_type = block.get("type")
   374→                            if block_type == "tool_use":
   375→                                has_tool_calls = True
   376→                                tool_id = block.get("id", "")
   377→                                tool_name = block.get("name", "")
   378→                                tool_input = block.get("input", {})
   379→                                tool_names[tool_id] = tool_name
   380→                                is_execute_sql = "execute_sql" in tool_name
   381→                                sql = tool_input.get("sql", "") if is_execute_sql else ""
   382→                                if sql:
   383→                                    tool_sqls[tool_id] = sql
   384→                                tool_call_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": tool_name}
   385→                                if sql:
   386→                                    tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   387→                                else:
   388→                                    tool_call_data["input"] = tool_input
   389→                                yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   390→                                if tool_name == "Task":
   391→                                    subagent_name = tool_input.get("subagent_type", "unknown")
   392→                                    subagent_prompt = tool_input.get("prompt", "")
   393→                                    tool_names[tool_id] = subagent_name
   394→                                    yield f"event: subagent_start\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': tool_id, 'name': subagent_name, 'prompt': subagent_prompt})}\n\n"
   395→
   396→                    # --- Tool results from user messages ---
   397→                    elif msg_type == "user":
   398→                        message_obj = msg.get("message", {})
   399→                        for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
   400→                            if block.get("type") != "tool_result":
   401→                                continue
   402→                            tool_id = block.get("tool_use_id", "")
   403→                            name = tool_names.get(tool_id, "")
   404→                            content_parts = block.get("content", [])
   405→                            text = ""
   406→                            if isinstance(content_parts, list):
   407→                                for part in content_parts:
   408→                                    if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get("type") == "text":
   409→                                        text = part.get("text", "")
   410→                            elif isinstance(content_parts, str):
   411→                                text = content_parts
   412→
   413→                            # Try to parse structured MCP result
   414→                            result_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
   415→                            # Include the SQL from the original tool_call
   416→                            original_sql = tool_sqls.get(tool_id, "")
   417→                            if original_sql:
   418→                                result_data["sql"] = original_sql
   419→                            try:
   420→                                parsed = json.loads(text)
   421→                                if parsed.get("status") == "success":
   422→                                    if "chart_spec" in parsed:
   423→                                        result_data["chart_spec"] = parsed["chart_spec"]
   424→                                    else:
   425→                                        result_data["columns"] = parsed.get("columns", [])
   426→                                        result_data["rows"] = parsed.get("rows", [])[:100]
   427→                                        result_data["rowCount"] = parsed.get("rowCount", 0)
   428→                                elif parsed.get("status") == "error":
   429→                                    result_data["error"] = parsed.get("error", "")
   430→                                else:
   431→                                    result_data["output"] = text
   432→                            except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   433→                                result_data["output"] = text
   434→                            if block.get("is_error"):
   435→                                try:
   436→                                    parsed_err = json.loads(text)
   437→                                    result_data["error"] = parsed_err.get("error", text)
   438→                                except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   439→                                    result_data["error"] = text
   440→                            # Detect subagent result (Task tool)
   441→                            if name in ("sql-analyst", "chart-builder"):
   442→                                end_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
   443→                                try:
   444→                                    parsed_sub = json.loads(text)
   445→                                    if "chart_spec" in parsed_sub:
   446→                                        end_data["chart_spec"] = parsed_sub["chart_spec"]
   447→                                    else:
   448→                                        end_data["result"] = text
   449→                                except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
   450→                                    end_data["result"] = text
   451→                                yield f"event: subagent_end\ndata: {json.dumps(end_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   452→                                continue
   453→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   454→
   455→                    # --- Final result ---
   456→                    elif msg_type == "result":
   457→                        actual_session_id = msg.get("session_id") or actual_session_id
   458→                        if msg.get("is_error"):
   459→                            errors = msg.get("errors", [])
   460→                            error_text = msg.get("result") or "; ".join(errors) or "Unknown error"
   461→                            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   462→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   463→                        done_sent = True
   464→
   465→                    # --- Sidecar error (e.g. SDK/CLI crash inside container) ---
   466→                    elif msg_type == "error":
   467→                        error_text = msg.get("message") or "Sidecar error"
   468→                        logger.error("Sidecar reported error: %s", error_text)
   469→                        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   470→
   471→                    # --- Extract session_id early from system init ---
   472→                    elif msg_type == "system":
   473→                        sys_session = msg.get("session_id")
   474→                        if sys_session:
   475→                            actual_session_id = sys_session
   476→
   477→        # Guard: always send done even if sidecar ended without result message
   478→        if not done_sent:
   479→            logger.warning("Sidecar stream ended without result message; sending done event")
   480→            yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   481→
   482→    except Exception as e:
   483→        logger.error("Container agent error: %s", str(e))
   484→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   485→    finally:
   486→        proxy_token_store.revoke_token(session_token)
   487→        # Container intentionally kept alive for session resume (--resume flag).
   488→        # Containers are cleaned up by the background cleanup loop after
   489→        # CONTAINER_MAX_LIFETIME_SECONDS, or on application shutdown.
   490→
   491→
   492→async def stream_chat(
   493→    message: str,
   494→    session_id: str | None = None,
   495→    db: Database | None = None,
   496→    conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None,
   497→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,
   498→    backend_session_id: str | None = None,
   499→) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   500→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
   501→    if CONTAINER_ENABLED:
   502→        from app.container_manager import container_manager
   503→        if container_manager is None:
   504→            logger.error(
   505→                "CONTAINER_ENABLED=true but Docker is not available. "
   506→                "Falling back to subprocess mode."
   507→            )
   508→        else:
   509→            async for event in _stream_chat_container(
   510→                message, session_id, db, conversation_history, container_manager,
   511→                backend_session_id=backend_session_id,
   512→                langfuse_session_id=langfuse_session_id,
   513→            ):
   514→                yield event
   515→            return
   516→
   517→    if db is None:
   518→        raise ValueError("db must be provided")
   519→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server(db)
   520→
   521→    logger.info("Using model: %s", ANTHROPIC_MODEL)
   522→
   523→    # Collect stderr from the CLI subprocess for debugging
   524→    stderr_lines: list[str] = []
   525→
   526→    # Use the --resume flag to continue an existing session
   527→    session_token=[REDACTED].create_token()
   528→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
   529→        model=ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
   530→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(db),
   531→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
   532→        allowed_tools=["Task", "mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
   533→        agents=build_subagent_definitions(db),
   534→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
   535→        max_turns=20,
   536→        include_partial_messages=True,
   537→        stderr=lambda line: stderr_lines.append(line),
   538→        env={
   539→            "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": session_token,
   540→            "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{PROXY_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   541→            # Scrub Langfuse credentials so the agent subprocess cannot
   542→            # read them from the inherited environment.
   543→            "LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY": "",
   544→            "LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY": "",
   545→        },
   546→        **({"resume": session_id} if session_id else {}),
   547→    )
   548→
   549→    # When editing, prepend conversation history to the user message
   550→    # instead of bloating the system prompt
   551→    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history)
   552→
   553→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
   554→    # Will be set from the CLI's ResultMessage; use the passed-in value until then
   555→    actual_session_id = session_id
   556→
   557→    # --- Langfuse OTel tracing setup (conditional) ---
   558→    # Deferred: session_id is set after the CLI returns it in ResultMessage
   559→    langfuse = get_langfuse_client()
   560→    observation_ctx = None
   561→    propagate_ctx = None
   562→    if langfuse:
   563→        try:
   564→            trace_input: dict = {"message": message[:500]}
   565→            if conversation_history:
   566→                trace_input["conversation_history"] = conversation_history
   567→            observation_ctx = langfuse.start_as_current_observation(
   568→                name="agent-chat",
   569→                input=trace_input,
   570→                metadata={"model": ANTHROPIC_MODEL},
   571→            )
   572→            observation_ctx.__enter__()
   573→
   574→            # Propagate the stable conversation session_id for child spans
   575→            effective_langfuse_session_id = langfuse_session_id or session_id
   576→            if effective_langfuse_session_id:
   577→                from langfuse import propagate_attributes
   578→                propagate_ctx = propagate_attributes(session_id=effective_langfuse_session_id)
   579→                propagate_ctx.__enter__()
   580→        except Exception as e:
   581→            logger.debug("Failed to set up Langfuse tracing context: %s", e)
   582→            observation_ctx = None
   583→
   584→    try:
   585→        await client.connect()
   586→        await client.query(query_message, session_id=session_id or "default")
   587→
   588→        has_tool_calls = False
   589→        has_thinking = False
   590→        done_sent = False
   591→        sql_result_ids: set[str] = set()
   592→        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}
   593→
   594→        async for msg in client.receive_response():
   595→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
   596→                event = msg.event
   597→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
   598→
   599→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   600→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
   601→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   602→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   603→                        text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   604→                        if text:
   605→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   606→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   607→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
   608→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
   609→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   610→
   611→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   612→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
   613→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   614→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   615→                        has_thinking = True
   616→                    elif block_type == "text":
   617→                        if has_thinking:
   618→                            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   619→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   620→                        has_thinking = False
   621→                        has_tool_calls = True
   622→
   623→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   624→                for block in msg.content:
   625→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   626→                        has_tool_calls = True
   627→                        tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "") or ""
   628→                        tool_names[block.id] = tool_name
   629→                        is_execute_sql = "execute_sql" in tool_name
   630→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "") if is_execute_sql else ""
   631→                        command = block.input.get("command", "")
   632→
   633→                        # Emit tool_call for ALL tool types
   634→                        tool_call_data: dict = {"id": block.id, "name": tool_name}
   635→                        if sql:
   636→                            tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   637→                        if command:
   638→                            tool_call_data["command"] = command
   639→                        if not sql and not command:
   640→                            tool_call_data["input"] = block.input
   641→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   642→
   643→                        # Detect subagent invocation via Task tool
   644→                        if tool_name == "Task":
   645→                            subagent_type = block.input.get("subagent_type", "unknown")
   646→                            tool_names[block.id] = subagent_type  # Store subagent name, not "Task"
   647→                            yield f"event: subagent_start\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': subagent_type, 'prompt': block.input.get('prompt', '')})}\n\n"
   648→
   649→                        # For execute_sql only, execute query for structured results
   650→                        if sql:
   651→                            sql_result_ids.add(block.id)
   652→                            try:
   653→                                result = db.execute_query(sql)
   654→                                truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   655→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   656→                            except Exception as e:
   657→                                yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'name': tool_name, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   658→
   659→            elif isinstance(msg, UserMessage):
   660→                # Capture tool results from the SDK for non-SQL tools
   661→                content = msg.content
   662→                if isinstance(content, list):
   663→                    for block in content:
   664→                        if isinstance(block, ToolResultBlock):
   665→                            if block.tool_use_id in sql_result_ids:
   666→                                continue
   667→                            output = _extract_tool_result_text(block.content)
   668→                            name = tool_names.get(block.tool_use_id, "")
   669→                            result_data: dict = {
   670→                                "id": block.tool_use_id,
   671→                                "name": name,
   672→                            }
   673→                            if block.is_error:
   674→                                try:
   675→                                    parsed_error = json.loads(output)
   676→                                    result_data["error"] = parsed_error.get("error", output)
   677→                                except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   678→                                    result_data["error"] = output
   679→                            else:
   680→                                # Try to parse JSON output (e.g. chart_spec from chart-builder subagent)
   681→                                try:
   682→                                    parsed = json.loads(output)
   683→                                    if parsed.get("status") == "success" and "chart_spec" in parsed:
   684→                                        result_data["chart_spec"] = parsed["chart_spec"]
   685→                                    else:
   686→                                        result_data["output"] = output
   687→                                except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   688→                                    result_data["output"] = output
   689→                            # Detect subagent result (Task tool)
   690→                            if name in ("sql-analyst", "chart-builder"):
   691→                                end_data: dict = {"id": block.tool_use_id, "name": name}
   692→                                chart_spec = _extract_chart_spec(output)
   693→                                if chart_spec:
   694→                                    end_data["chart_spec"] = chart_spec
   695→                                else:
   696→                                    end_data["result"] = output
   697→                                yield f"event: subagent_end\ndata: {json.dumps(end_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   698→                                continue  # Don't also emit tool_result for Task
   699→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   700→
   701→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   702→                # Use the CLI's session_id so --resume can find it
   703→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id or actual_session_id
   704→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   705→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   706→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   707→                done_sent = True
   708→
   709→        # Guard: always send done even if SDK ended without ResultMessage
   710→        if not done_sent:
   711→            logger.warning("SDK stream ended without ResultMessage; sending done event")
   712→            yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   713→
   714→    except Exception as e:
   715→        error_msg = str(e)
   716→        if stderr_lines:
   717→            error_msg += f" | CLI stderr: {' '.join(stderr_lines[-5:])}"
   718→        logger.error("Agent error: %s", error_msg)
   719→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_msg})}\n\n"
   720→        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   721→    finally:
   722→        proxy_token_store.revoke_token(session_token)
   723→        # Update trace with the CLI's session_id so Langfuse session matches
   724→        if langfuse and observation_ctx:
   725→            try:
   726→                trace_session_id = langfuse_session_id or actual_session_id
   727→                langfuse.update_current_trace(
   728→                    session_id=trace_session_id,
   729→                    output={"session_id": actual_session_id},
   730→                )
   731→                if propagate_ctx:
   732→                    propagate_ctx.__exit__(None, None, None)
   733→                observation_ctx.__exit__(None, None, None)
   734→            except Exception as e:
   735→                logger.debug("Failed to finalize Langfuse trace: %s", e)
   736→            try:
   737→                langfuse.flush()
   738→            except Exception as e:
   739→                logger.debug("Failed to flush Langfuse: %s", e)
   740→
   741→        try:
   742→            await client.disconnect()
   743→        except Exception:
   744→            pass
   745→
```

> AGENT

Bug fix is complete. Here's a summary of what was done:

**Root cause**: The chart-builder subagent returns markdown text with an embedded JSON code block (e.g., `` ```json {...} ``` ``), not pure JSON. The previous code used `json.loads(output)` directly, which fails on markdown text, so `chart_spec` was never extracted and the raw JSON appeared as text in the UI instead of rendering as a Plotly chart.

**Fix**:
1. Added `_extract_chart_spec(text)` helper in `backend/app/agent.py` that:
   - Tries parsing as pure JSON first (for backwards compatibility)
   - Falls back to regex extraction from markdown fenced code blocks (`` ```json ``` `` or `` ``` ``` ``)
   - Handles both `{"chart_spec": {...}}` wrapper and direct `{"data": [...], "layout": {...}}` format
2. Updated both **local subprocess mode** and **container mode** subagent_end emission to use the new helper
3. Moved `import re` to module level
4. Added 8 unit tests covering all parsing scenarios — all 101 tests pass

Remove ```json ... ``` blocks containing chart_spec\n * or Plotly trace data so they don't appear as raw text.\n */\nfunction stripChartSpecBlocks(text: string): string {\n  return text.replace(/```(?:json)?\\s*\\n?\\s*\\{[\\s\\S]*?\"(?:chart_spec|data)\"[\\s\\S]*?\\}\\s*\\n?\\s*```/g, '').trim();\n}\n\nfunction getLastThinkingLine"

Done. Here's what changed:

**Backend** (`agent.py`): Added prompt instruction telling the orchestrator to not repeat chart spec JSON after the chart-builder returns — just describe the visualization in plain language.

**Frontend** (`MessageBubble.tsx`):
1. Added `stripChartSpecBlocks()` — regex strips `` ```json {"chart_spec":...} ``` `` blocks from answer text as a safety net
2. Charts now render **inside** the answer section under a single "Answer" label, instead of in a separate section above it
3. Answer text segments are filtered through the stripper when charts are present, so any residual JSON is removed
